HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0b1cba7300050f055e0d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:2:2\",\"root_001219\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Felaket, bir sözlük tanımıyla değil, insanlarda ve dağlarda oluşturduğu dönüşümler üzerinden anlaşılmaya açılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_34570b13dadd4d27ffbf","text":"3. **Exploratory selection at a threshold.** `ق ر ع B004` covers deciding by lots and drawing shares, while 101:6 introduces the one whose balances become heavy and 101:7 gives that person's resulting condition. The move from universal disruption to `فَأَمَّا مَن` structurally begins an allocation. The branch gives `ٱلْقَارِعَةُ` the role of threshold at which outcomes start to be assigned. The packet gives weighing rather than lot-casting, so equating the procedures would be unjustified; the abductive claim is only that both instantiate decisive apportionment. The question can thus open onto \"what sorting event is this?\" alongside the impact reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4439d5b0152e5a34baac","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:2:2\",\"root_001219\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Belirsiz soru, insanların üzerine saçılacağı açık bir sahne kuruyormuş gibi de okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4d32f03561595a8cad9c","text":"The causative knowing formula in 101:3 corrected a purely definitional reading of this question: the answer will be produced through scenes rather than a synonym. The material effects in 101:4-5 activated `ق ر ع B001`, while 101:6-7 made `ق ر ع B004` newly visible as an exploratory allocation mechanism. The late shift from all people to one weighed person is the decisive structural cue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8a655a2f959424dd93c8","text":"The later paired outcomes in 101:6-9 made the interrogative ما feel less like mere wonder and more like pre-adjudicative suspense. 101:11 also showed that the question-space is not filled by explanation but by a terse material answer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b242b7936a9fd1769a59","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:2:2\",\"root_001219\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlanan soru bilgi istemekten çok ilk darbeyi zihinde yeniden vurarak erken bir tanımı engeller.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b7b6dc6ef55606b81058","text":"1. **A question that repeats the strike cognitively.** `ق ر ع B006` supplies the striking warning, and the interrogative `مَا` immediately returns the isolated noun of 101:1 rather than resolving it. The question assigns the branch a cognitive role: the named event now strikes settled understanding and restrains premature closure. I infer this hearer-directed effect from question-after-repetition; grammar supplies the interruption, while its checking force is the abductive move. The ayah reads not merely as a request for a definition but as a second impact enacted in inquiry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b8edd04360dbb94d8495","text":"2. **Impact understood by its transformations.** Through `ق ر ع B001`, the following people/moth and mountain/wool similes in 101:4-5 become the material answer to the question. Their parallel syntax gives the striking branch a cross-domain role, converting both coordinated crowds and massive terrain into dispersed or loosened matter. The unstated move is that these changes are effects of the named event. The question therefore reads as an invitation to infer the event from what it does, rather than to replace it with an abstract gloss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cd6e23a248649b116508","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:2:2\",\"root_001219\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ağır ve hafif sonuçlardan önceki belirsizlik, soruyu hüküm verilmeden önceki paylaştırma eşiğine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001","root_001219/B004","root_001219/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f7fdcf50bf85a950ccb7","text":"1. Question as magnifying impact. Evidence: ق ر ع B001/B006; د ر ي B001 appears in the next ayah. Structural cue: bare nominal repetition is now preceded by ما. Mechanism: the question does not request ordinary information; it makes the strike reverberate by withholding definition. Abductive move: I infer that repetition plus interrogation intensifies rather than explains. Reading change: \"what is al-Qari'ah\" reads as a second knock, not a neutral query. 2. The event as unassigned share. Evidence: ق ر ع B004, lots and shares; و ز ن B001/B002 later, weighing and justice. Structural cue: ما leaves the referent undetermined before the later measured division. Mechanism: unknownness marks the event before its outcomes have been distributed. Abductive move: I supply an analogy between interrogative indeterminacy and lot/scale indeterminacy before decision. Reading change: the ayah becomes the suspense before apportionment: the share has not yet fallen. 3. The open road/courtyard as question-space. Evidence: ق ر ع B010, exposed road/courtyard; ف ر ش B001/B002 in 101:4, spreading and prepared surface. Structural cue: the question is followed by human dispersal across a broad scene. Mechanism: the undefined قارعة opens a visible field into which people will be spread. Abductive move: I infer spatial expansion from the later similes. Reading change: ما القارعة reads as opening an arena rather than merely asking for a definition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
